Pasted text is treated as metadata only; no pasted code is evaluated.

- Objective: close the browser/runtime proof gap for the owner-approved aggressive Three.js visualization launch after converting 26 approved families and 80 premium topic pages.

No API routes, generated-content packages, app shell, package/config files, git state, staging, committing, branch, push, reset, clean, delete, or revert actions were performed.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/configuredThreeDRenderPlan.ts` now infers launch metadata from `labId` for premium/capstone Three.js pages, including `coverageTier`, `premiumLaunch`, and `regionalPriority`; `ConfiguredVisualizationLab.tsx` passes the plan metadata into `ThreeDLabCanvas`.

- Coverage evidence: generated-content-independent launch contracts prove 26 Three.js families, 80 premium topic pages, and approved regional counts of Mainland 40, California 12, Hong Kong 9, and cross-region 19.

## MAIS Manim V2 Source-Architecture Layer - 13:07 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: implement MAIS Manim 第二版 using the owner-referenced `3blue1brown-math-visualization-skill` and its Manim source-code architecture distillation, specifically adding Manim-like semantic runtime state instead of only raw R3F rendering.
- Skill evidence used: `/Users/dongpinhu/.codex/skills/3blue1brown-math-visualization-skill/SKILL.md` and `references/manim-source-architecture.md`; key source-level rules applied were `MathObject` wrappers, scene-level timeline state, updater registry, camera director, trackers, coordinate-system-driven objects, formula bindings, and testable runtime evidence.
- TDD RED: added `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneRuntimeState.test.ts`; first run failed with `Cannot find module './mathSceneRuntimeState'`, proving the V2 modules were absent.
- Changed: added `mathSceneRuntimeState.ts`, building a Manim-inspired object graph with semantic IDs, parent/child links, bounding boxes, render states, timeline trackers, updater diagnostics, and camera-director state.
- Changed: added `mathUpdaterRegistry.ts`, a pure updater registry for `reveal-curve`, `move-along-path`, and `trace-recent-path`, with deterministic moving point and trace render-state evaluation.
- Changed: added `mathCameraDirector.ts`, a pure camera-director contract with canonical/reset shot IDs, active shot selection from `cameraTo` beats, and stable camera transition progress.
- Changed: `MathSceneRuntime.tsx` now consumes `buildMathSceneRuntimeState()` and `applyMathUpdaters()` so R3F rendering is driven by semantic runtime state rather than independent per-object timeline calculations.
- Changed: `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx` now exposes V2 QA fields: `data-viz-math-object-count`, `data-viz-tracker-count`, `data-viz-updater-count`, `data-viz-camera-canonical-shot`, `data-viz-reduced-motion`, and per-object `data-viz-concept-id`; `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` includes these fields for S11/S22 checks.
- Changed: `configuredThreeDRenderPlan.test.ts` now uses `statistics-distribution` as the primitive fallback sample because the current S06 worktree has already migrated several senior math families to MAIS Manim.

- Objective: fix the user-reported bug on `/visualization-lab/stembench-euler-demo` where the focused vertex could not be moved with the mouse.
- Root cause: the original SVG vertex visual target was smaller than the browser's large default focus outline, and drag reliability depended on pointer events landing on the painted circles. A user clicking the apparent focus box could miss the actual draggable SVG painted region.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/StembenchEulerLineDemo.tsx` now gives every vertex an invisible `pointerEvents="all"` hit circle with radius `31`, suppresses the browser's oversized default outline on the SVG button group, and keeps the demo's own circular active highlight/focus state.
- Existing repair confirmed: drag state is held in `dragRef` with SVG pointer capture, so pointer movement is no longer blocked by async React state or by leaving the small painted handle.
- Runtime note: the local Next dev server on port `3057` was restarted because its generated `.next` dev cache was returning React Client Manifest / missing chunk 500 errors. The route then returned `200 OK`.
- Verification passed: Playwright center-drag of vertex C changed `data-report-og` from `34.97` to `27.73` and recorded `started` plus `adjusted` events.
- Verification passed: Playwright edge-hit drag from `26` SVG units outside C's center hit `Drag vertex C`, changed `data-report-og` from `27.73` to `33.22`, and recorded `started` plus `adjusted` events.
- Verification passed: screenshot `/tmp/stembench-euler-drag-fixed.png` showed the focused C vertex with the intended circular highlight and no giant browser focus rectangle.
- Verification passed: `git diff --check -- components/visualizations/StembenchEulerLineDemo.tsx app/visualization-lab/stembench-euler-demo/page.tsx coordination/session-logs/2026-06-20-S06.md`.
- Verification blocked: `npm run type-check -- --pretty false` failed in unrelated backend/userStore parent-notice test work: `lib/server/userStoreParentNoticePersistence.test.ts` imports missing `@/lib/server/userStore/parentNoticePersistence` and has implicit-any parameters. S06 did not modify that S12/S14-owned area.
- Git note: no branch, stage, commit, push, reset, clean, delete, or revert was performed.

## MAIS Manim V2 Named CameraFrame Shot Authoring Slice - 23:05 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 by filling the Manim source-distillation "named camera shots" playground gap with pure shot-catalog evidence and a browser authoring control.
- TDD RED: added `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathCameraShotAuthoring.test.ts` and updated `components/visualizations/three/threeDCanvasContract.test.ts`; first focused run failed because `./mathCameraShotAuthoring` and its canvas attributes/control did not exist.
- Changed: added `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathCameraShotAuthoring.ts`, a pure module that builds a named CameraFrame shot catalog from `MathSceneSpec.cameraShots` plus authored `cameraTo` timeline beats.
- Changed: the catalog maps canonical shots to elapsed `0` and timeline shots to the first matching `cameraTo` beat start, e.g. function graph `overview` at `0` and `curve-detail` at `8.64`.
- Changed: `cameraShotCatalogDataAttributes()` exposes `data-viz-manim-camera-shot-count`, `data-viz-manim-camera-shot-ids`, `data-viz-manim-camera-shot-selected`, and `data-viz-manim-camera-shot-summary`.
- Changed: `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx` now memoizes the shot catalog, exposes those four root attributes with primitive fallbacks, and adds a compact `data-viz-manim-camera-shot-control` select beside the guided/explore camera controls.
- Changed: `jumpToManimCameraShot()` switches the scene to guided camera mode, pauses playback, jumps to the selected shot's authored elapsed time, advances the frame index, and records the jump in Manim authoring history.
- Changed: `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` includes the four camera-shot attributes plus `data-viz-manim-camera-shot-control` in the S11/S22 smoke surface contract.
